# Blinded dual-review material (protocol v6 item 4)

Generated by `blind_reviews.py`. Each pair's two reviewers appear as **RA** and
**RB** under a mapping drawn independently for that pair, so RA in one pair says
nothing about RA in the next. Model names, draw bytes, parity records and block
and slot records are redacted; the per-reviewer cost and rubric cells are not
reproduced at all.

**You are the coder. Do not open `docs/MODEL-AB-LOG.md`, and do not open
`analysis/model-ab/keys/`.** Either one dissolves the blind, and the fourth
readout's lesson — coding and adjudication ran unblinded, in a session of one of
the two models under comparison — is the reason item 4 exists. Record your own
model in the readout.

Code each finding against the item-3 instrument, which is normative and lives in
`docs/MODEL-AB-LOG.md`'s protocol v6 entry. A finding enters the tally iff ALL
of: (a) UNILATERAL — raised as MAJOR by one reviewer and never mentioned by the
other at any severity; (b) DEFECT CLASS code, test-gap or contract-API — doc- or
claim-only findings are recorded but excluded; (c) DEDUP — findings tracing to
one underlying defect count once; (d) DEMONSTRATED BY EXECUTION — a red probe, a
surviving mutant gone red, a compile-fail pin, a red CI row, or a measured wrong
value; (e) FAIR PAIR — a pair where either review was interrupted or truncated
is excluded from the tally though still recorded, and unrecoverable counts are
missing data, never zeros.

**The rows carry the orchestrators' own verdicts, and they are DATA, not your
coding.** Most cells declare a tally line ("v6 tally after this pair: +1
candidate"), and many label a finding TALLY CANDIDATE, UNILATERAL, bilateral or
CONVERGED. These are deliberately left in: they are the record of what the
orchestrator concluded at merge, and the readout's subject is partly whether
that record holds up. Read them as claims to audit against the finding
descriptions beside them, never as an answer to inherit — the log's own running
tally is stated inconsistently by four different rows and then abandoned, which
is why this pass exists. Where your coding departs from a row's declared verdict,
code what you find and say so in the evidence column.

Write findings into the blank forms beside this file. `raiser` takes `A` or `B`,
never a slot or a model.

Pairs: 153.

- **unit**: PERF-6 (PR #2339; spec docs/PERF-6-SPEC.md; executes Ev's finding `tier3-plus-v-needs-a-sign-and-pays-for-a-precision`, PR 2303): tier 3's +V check certifies a sign, not a precision — `topo::props::SignCertificate<'b, T>` (no `volume` field, D9 row 0; `enclosure()`, `refine_to_target()`, `target_refusal()`) beside the unchanged `MassProperties<T>` as the `Target` level; `quad.rs`'s three lanes entered over a `RoundWindow { first, last }` with `RoundOutcome::{Converged, Open}` and the public doors kept as one-line wrappers; `sign_certified` runs round 0 for every face in arena order, decides on `volume_lo` (`positive_volume_enclosure`, a new named predicate) after `positive_volume` on `volume_hi`, and advances every open face one round until settled or the target; the reporting door's bits pinned by a digest cut before the change. Fix pass: union of 17 items — the undecided-with-exhausted-schedule hole closed by construction (`sign_certified` returns a verdict beside the certificate; `PlusVVerdict::Uncomputable(source)` carries the reporting door's own refusal; `PlusVSubject` deleted; a thin-strip row in both senses at three ε), the digest roster extended to every certified lane with an ε-coupled body (a quintic loft on the composite rounds, a tilted cut on the cylinder Green form; cut on the merge base, byte-identical at three ε), predicates minted once per body again, the continuation short-circuiting as the reporting walk does, the true cost stated (the sign door reads every face every round; gate + continuation 1.3–1.8× one measurement from per-window setup re-entry — filed `quadrature-setup-is-re-derived-per-round-window`), the `lo` reconstruction's rounding argued quantitatively, one `round_exit` helper for three lanes, `quad_verdicts` into `tests/common`, the stale-premise class swept by prose, the ledger paragraph re-worded (the certificate is public) with `validate.rs`'s count back at its ratified 9; filed `sense-inversion-is-invisible-to-tier-3-on-arc-capped-lofts`. Measured: `loft_prism` tier 3 unchanged (~155 ms; the exact per-span arm takes no rounds); an arc loft at 1e9·ε 2379 → 768 ms; at 1e11·ε +10–12 % (the sign door cannot stop at a refusing face).
